Tests run under pytest.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"database-target\" stories=\"PERSIST-001\">\n`DATABASE_URL` names the database. A value beginning `postgres://` or `postgresql://` is a PostgreSQL DSN. Any other value is a SQLite file path, optionally prefixed `sqlite://`. When `DATABASE_URL` is unset, `DATABASE_PATH` is used, then `data/app.db`.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"three-table-schema\" stories=\"PERSIST-002\">\nThree tables. Amounts are stored as integer cents and never as floating point.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"access-through-persistence-library\" stories=\"PERSIST-002,PERSIST-001\">\nAll database access goes through the shipped persistence library and the relational layer generated from `DATABASE.md`. Application code never opens a database driver directly.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"atomic-commands\" stories=\"INTEG-001\">\n**Every command is one database transaction.** A command that fails for any reason leaves every table exactly as it was.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"non-negative-balance\" stories=\"INTEG-002\">\n**No command leaves an account balance below zero.** An account's balance is the sum of its transaction amounts.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"code-format\" stories=\"VALID-001\">\n**Codes** (`account`, `category`) are 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"name-and-memo-length\" stories=\"VALID-001\">\n**Names and descriptions** are 1 to 40 characters. **Memos** are 0 to 60 characters. None of them contains a comma or a newline.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"amount-format\" stories=\"VALID-002\">\n**Amounts** on input match `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`: at most two decimal places, never zero. On output an amount is written with exactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative, and no thousands separator: `-12.50`, `0.00`, `5000.00`.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"date-format\" stories=\"VALID-003\">\n**Dates** are real calendar dates written `YYYY-MM-DD`. `2026-02-30` is rejected.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"keep-loaded-txn-id\" stories=\"LOAD-003\">\nA loaded transaction keeps the `txn_id` from its file.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"post-next-txn-id\" stories=\"TXN-001\">\nA posted transaction receives one more than the largest `txn_id` present, or `1` in an empty table.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"transfer-linked-txn-ids\" stories=\"XFER-001\">\nA transfer's outgoing row takes that id and its incoming row the next.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"init-schema\" stories=\"PERSIST-003\">\n`ledger init` | Drops all three tables if present and creates them empty. Nothing written.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"count-rows\" stories=\"PERSIST-004\">\n`ledger count` | `table,rows`, then `accounts`, `categories`, `transactions` rows in that order.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"load-table\" stories=\"LOAD-001,LOAD-002,LOAD-003\">\n`ledger load <table> <csv>` | Loads `accounts`, `categories`, or `transactions` from a file whose header matches the table's columns in the order above. Writes `table,loaded` and `<table>,<rows>`.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"csv-success-output\" stories=\"CLI-002\">\nEvery successful command except `init` writes CSV to standard output: one header row, then data rows, comma-separated, no quoting, no trailing spaces, `\\n` line endings.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"failure-diagnostic\" stories=\"CLI-002\">\nA failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"account-row-format\" stories=\"ACCT-002,RPT-001\">\nThe account row is `account,name,transactions,balance`, where `transactions` counts the account's transactions.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"transaction-row-format\" stories=\"TXN-001,TXN-002\">\nThe transaction row is `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"account-add\" stories=\"ACCT-001\">\n`ledger account add <code> <name>` | The new account row.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"account-get\" stories=\"ACCT-002\">\n`ledger account get <code>` | The account row.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"account-rename\" stories=\"ACCT-003\">\n`ledger account rename <code> <name>` | The updated account row.\n</requirement>\n<requirement name=\"account-delete\" stories=\"ACCT-004\">\n`ledger account delete <code>` | `deleted` and the code.
